### [132] [Fluxes of Generic Extreme-Mass-Ratio Inspirals with a Spinning Secondary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18075)
*Qiuxin Cui,Wen-Biao Han*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 构建了极端质量比旋进系统中次级天体自旋对引力波波形影响的线性自旋近似模型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端质量比旋进系统是未来空间引力波观测的关键目标，将次级天体自旋纳入波形模型不仅能提高测量精度，还能提供恒星质量天体自旋分布的见解

Method: 在Kerr背景下采用线性自旋近似，使用辐射处方（半推迟场减半超前场）推导了包含能量、角动量、类Carter常数和平行自旋分量的基本运动常数的轨道平均演化方程

Result: 建立了包含次级天体自旋的波形生成框架，为未来简化工作提供了可行路径

Conclusion: 该工作为极端质量比旋进系统中次级天体自旋效应的建模提供了理论基础，有助于未来引力波观测中自旋分布的精确测量

Abstract: Extreme mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs), comprising a stellar-mass compact object (CO) orbiting a supermassive black hole (BH), are key targets for future space-based gravitational-wave (GW) observatories. Incorporating the spin of the secondary body into waveform models not only enhances measurement precision but also offers insight into the spin distribution of stellar-mass objects. In this work, we construct the flux and waveform for an EMRI with a spinning secondary in a Kerr background under the linear-spin approximation. Using the radiative prescription (half-retarded minus half-advanced field), we derive orbit-averaged evolution equations for the fundamental constants of motion, including the energy, angular momentum, Carter-like constant, and the parallel spin component. This framework provides a tractable route to generating waveforms that incorporate the secondary spin, with the potential for further simplification in future work.

</details>


### [133] [Discovery of Bimodal Drift Rate Structure in FRB 20240114A: Evidence for Dual Emission Regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18109)
*Santosh Arron*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 在FRB 20240114A的上漂移爆发簇中发现了双峰结构的漂移率分布，揭示了两类具有显著不同物理特性的爆发簇亚群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索快速射电暴中爆发簇的漂移率分布特征，以揭示其发射机制和可能的物理起源，特别是寻找是否存在多个不同的发射区域。

Method: 使用无监督机器学习方法（UMAP降维结合HDBSCAN密度聚类）分析FAST望远镜的233个上漂移爆发簇数据，通过高斯混合模型检验双峰性，并计算统计显著性指标。

Result: 发现了45个爆发簇组成的极端漂移亚群（C1），其平均漂移率是典型上漂移爆发簇的2.5倍（245.6 vs 98.1 MHz/ms）。双峰性统计证据强烈（Δ-BIC=296.6），两模态分离明显（Ashman's D=2.70），分布中存在显著间隙（11.3σ）。该亚群还具有更低的峰值频率（-7%）、更短的持续时间（-29%）和独特的特征空间聚类模式。

Conclusion: 上漂移爆发簇的漂移率分布存在明确的双峰结构，表明可能存在两个空间分离的磁层发射区域，各自产生具有不同物理特性的爆发簇。这一发现为理解FRB的发射机制提供了新线索，但需要更多观测数据来确认。

Abstract: We report the discovery of bimodal structure in the drift rate distribution of upward-drifting burst clusters from the hyperactive repeating fast radio burst FRB 20240114A. Using unsupervised machine learning (UMAP dimensionality reduction combined with HDBSCAN density-based clustering) applied to 233 upward-drifting burst clusters from the FAST telescope dataset, we identify a distinct subpopulation of 45 burst clusters (Cluster C1) with mean drift rates 2.5x higher than typical upward-drifting burst clusters (245.6 vs 98.1 MHz/ms). Gaussian mixture modeling reveals strong evidence for bimodality (delta-BIC = 296.6), with clearly separated modes (Ashman's D = 2.70 > 2) and a statistically significant gap in the distribution (11.3 sigma). Crucially, we demonstrate that this bimodality persists when restricting the analysis to single-component (U1) burst clusters only (delta-BIC = 19.9, Ashman's D = 2.71), confirming that the result is not an artifact of combining single- and multi-component burst clusters with different drift rate definitions. The extreme-drift subpopulation also exhibits systematically lower peak frequencies (-7%), shorter durations (-29%), and distinct clustering in multi-dimensional feature space. These findings are suggestive of two spatially separated emission regions in the magnetosphere, each producing upward-drifting burst clusters with distinct physical characteristics, although confirmation requires observations from additional epochs and sources.

</details>


### [134] [Probing Kerr black hole in a uniform Bertotti-Robinson magnetic field through astrophysical quasi-periodic oscillations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18129)
*Hamza Rehman,Sanjar Shaymatov,Saddam Hussain,Tao Zhu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 研究探讨了Kerr黑洞在均匀Bertotti-Robinson磁场中的高频准周期振荡行为，通过贝叶斯推断和MCMC分析约束黑洞参数，发现磁场参数虽小但不可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究磁场对黑洞周围粒子动力学和准周期振荡的影响，通过观测数据约束黑洞参数，探索磁场在黑洞X射线双星系统中的物理作用。

Method: 分析测试粒子在磁场Kerr黑洞中的测地线方程，计算轨道、径向和垂直向心频率；基于参数共振和强迫共振机制构建QPO理论模型；使用多个黑洞X射线双星的观测数据，通过贝叶斯推断和马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛分析约束黑洞参数。

Result: 对于GRO J1655-40、GRS 1915+105、H1743-322和M82~X-1，在参数共振模型下获得了非零的无量纲磁场参数b（68%置信水平）；其他源仅获得90%置信水平的上限；在强迫共振模型中，所有源仅获得磁场参数的90%置信水平上限。磁场参数虽小但不可忽略，对粒子动力学和向心频率产生轻微修正。

Conclusion: 磁场对黑洞周围粒子动力学和准周期振荡有可观测的影响，参数共振模型比强迫共振模型更支持非零磁场参数的存在，磁场效应虽小但需要在黑洞物理模型中予以考虑。

Abstract: In this study, the behavior of high-frequency quasi-periodic oscillations (QPOs) is investigated around a Kerr black hole immersed in a uniform Bertotti-Robinson magnetic field. The motion of the test particle is analyzed by determining the geodesic equations and evaluating the corresponding orbital, radial, and vertical epicyclic frequencies. These fundamental frequencies are used to construct the theoretical framework of QPO models based on parametric and forced resonance mechanisms. Observational data obtained from several black hole X-ray binaries (GRO J1655-40, XTE J1550-564, XTE J1859+226, GRS 1915+105, H1743-322, M82~X-1, and Sgr~A$^{*}$) are used to constrain the black hole parameters through Bayesian inference and Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) analyses. For the X-ray binaries GRO J1655-40, GRS 1915+105, H1743-322, and M82~X-1, nonzero values of the dimensionless parameter $b=Bm$ are obtained at the $68\%$ confidence level within the framework of the parametric resonance model, while only upper bounds at the $90\%$ confidence level are obtained for the remaining sources. In contrast, in the case of the forced resonance model, only an upper bound at the $90\%$ confidence interval is obtained for the magnetic field parameter for all considered X-ray binary sources. The analysis indicates that the value of the magnetic field parameter is small but not negligible, producing minor modifications to particle dynamics and epicyclic frequencies. The influence of the magnetic field is further examined through the properties of the innermost stable circular orbit and the radiative properties of the thin accretion disk, including the energy flux and temperature profiles, within the allowed parameter range inferred from the MCMC analysis.

</details>


### [135] [Spectral Hardness as the Primary Discriminator: Unveiling the Collapsar--Merger Boundary with a Gold-Standard Gamma-Ray Burst Sample](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18150)
*Xue Zhang,Yan-Kun Qu,Shuang-Xi Yi,Yu-Peng Yang,Fen Lyu,Fa-Yin Wang,Zhong-Xiao Man*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 基于24个有光谱确认前身星（超新星或千新星）的GRB金标准样本，使用支持向量机建立物理驱动的分类方法，通过分离主尖峰排除扩展发射污染，得到定量分类指数I_SVM，将I_SVM>0的事件分类为并合事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立基于物理参数的稳健GRB分类方法，区分并合事件（中子星-中子星或中子星-黑洞并合）和塌缩事件（大质量恒星塌缩），以解决传统基于T90持续时间分类的局限性，为精确宇宙学提供清洁样本。

Method: 使用支持向量机在24个有光谱确认前身星的金标准GRB样本上进行训练，通过分离主尖峰排除扩展发射污染，提取三个关键参数：峰值能量E_p,i、各向同性能量E_iso和红移修正持续时间T_90,z，建立定量分类指数I_SVM。

Result: 得到分类指数I_SVM = 5.01 log_10 E_p,i - 1.25 log_10 E_iso - 0.34 log_10 T_90,z - 12.90，其中I_SVM>0为并合事件。分析显示E_p,i的判别能力约为T_90,z的5倍，E_iso贡献权重与E_p,i相当，表明谱硬度和能量学而非持续时间是区分并合与塌缩的主要物理特征。

Conclusion: 该方法成功识别历史测试案例（如超长GRB 111209A为塌缩事件，短GRB 050709为并合事件），独立于仪器时代，为清理档案和未来高红移GRB样本提供了物理驱动的分类工具，有助于精确宇宙学研究。

Abstract: In this Letter, we establish a robust, physically motivated classification method using a Support Vector Machine (SVM) trained on a "gold-standard" sample of 24 GRBs with spectroscopically confirmed progenitors (associated SNe or KNe). By isolating the prompt main spike to excise contamination from extended emission, we derive a quantitative classification index, I_SVM = 5.01 log_10 E_p,i - 1.25 log_10 E_iso - 0.34 log_10 T_90,z - 12.90 (units: keV, 10^52 erg, s). Events with I_SVM > 0 are classified as mergers. Analysis of the standardized classification weights reveals that the discriminative power of E_p,i is approximately 5 times that of T_90,z, while E_iso contributes a weight comparable to E_p,i. This quantitatively demonstrates that spectral hardness and energetics, rather than duration, are the primary physical signatures distinguishing mergers from collapsars. The derived boundary implies a stringent hardness ceiling for collapsars, while mergers are identified as outliers with excessive hardness relative to their energy budget. The classifier successfully identifies the nature of historic test cases, including the ultra-long GRB 111209A (collapsar) and the short GRB 050709 (merger), independent of instrumental eras. This tool paves the way for cleaning archival and future high-redshift GRB samples for precision cosmology.

</details>


### [136] [Supermassive Black Hole Winds in X-rays: SUBWAYS V. Properties of hot coronae in quasars at intermediate redshift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18156)
*S. Peluso,G. Lanzuisi,A. Comastri,M. Brusa,M. Giustini,G. Miniutti,S. Bianchi,V. E. Gianolli,R. Middei,P-O. Petrucci,L. Borrelli,E. Amenta,E. Bertola,B. De Marco,A. De Rosa,S. Kraemer,G. Kriss,Y. Krongold,S. Mathur,A. Merloni,E. Nardini,F. Panessa,E. Piconcelli,G. Ponti,F. Ricci,A. Tortosa,L. Zappacosta,R. Serafinelli*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 通过X射线分析23个射电宁静AGN的日冕特性，发现高光度AGN具有更冷、光学厚度更大的日冕，高能截止能量与爱丁顿比、光度呈显著反相关


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究活动星系核(AGN)中热日冕的特性，特别是通过硬X射线波段发射来探究日冕性质，并考虑软X射线波段的适当处理。重点关注中等红移(0.1<z<0.4)和高光度(2×10⁴⁴<L_bol<2×10⁴⁶ erg/s)的类星体样本。

Method: 使用SUBWAYS项目的23个AGN样本，结合XMM-Newton的高质量X射线光谱和NuSTAR数据(扩展到30-40 keV静止框架)。软X射线波段(0.3-2 keV)采用暖日冕模型拟合，硬X射线波段采用热日冕模型。将结果与低红移和高红移的文献样本结合，构建了迄今最大的具有E_cut和吸积参数测量的AGN样本。

Result: 软X射线波段最佳拟合为暖日冕模型，中位温度0.40 keV，光学深度τ=20-40。硬X射线波段由热日冕模型描述，中位高能截止能量87 keV，处于赛弗特星系典型值(100-200 keV)的低端。光学深度τ=1-5表明存在中等光学厚度的日冕。E_cut与爱丁顿比λ_Edd和光度L_bol呈显著反相关：低吸积率和光度时E_cut为250-300 keV，高吸积率和光度时降至90-100 keV。

Conclusion: 高光度AGN相比低光度赛弗特星系具有更冷、光学厚度更大的日冕。E_cut与吸积参数的负相关支持了增强的日冕冷却机制，可能由正负电子对产生驱动。这些结果对理解AGN日冕物理和吸积过程具有重要意义。

Abstract: We present the X-ray analysis of coronal properties in a statistically representative sample of 23 mostly radio-quiet AGN from the SUBWAYS campaign (SUpermassive Black holes Winds in XrAYs), focusing on quasars at redshifts $0.1 < z < 0.4 $ and bolometric luminosities $2 \times 10^{44} <L_{bol}(erg/s) < 2 \times 10^{46}$. The main aim of this work is to investigate the properties of the hot corona through the study of the hard X-ray band emission, including a proper treatment of the soft X-ray band. High-quality X-ray spectra from XMM-Newton, complemented by NuSTAR data extending up to 30-40 keV in the rest frame, are available for this sample. The soft X-ray band (0.3-2 keV) spectrum is best fitted by a warm corona model with a median temperature of 0.40 keV, and an optical depth in the range $τ$=20 - 40, consistent with previous results on lower luminosity sources. The hard X-ray band is well described using a hot corona model, with a median high-energy cut-off of 87 keV, at the lower end of the distribution of typical values found in Seyfert galaxies (100 - 200 keV). The derived median value of the optical depth ($τ$ = 1 - 5) suggests the presence of a moderately optically thick corona. Combining the SUBWAYS results with literature samples at low and high redshift, we assemble the largest sample to date of AGN with E$_{cut}$ and accretion parameter measurements, finding a significant anticorrelation of E$_{cut}$ with both $λ_{Edd}$ and $L_{bol}$ with the median E$_{cut}$ decreasing from 250 - 300 keV at low accretion rates and luminosities to 90 - 100 keV at high accretion rates and luminosities - consistent with enhanced coronal cooling, possibly driven by pair-production. These results favor cooler, optically thicker coronae in luminous AGN compared to those in lower-luminosity Seyfert galaxies.

</details>


### [137] [Neutrinos and gamma rays from Seyfert galaxies constrain the properties of coronal turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18214)
*Federico Testagrossa,Damiano F. G. Fiorillo,Luca Comisso,Enrico Peretti,Maria Petropoulou,Lorenzo Sironi*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 利用NGC 1068和NGC 7469的多信使观测数据，通过联合拟合IceCube中微子和Fermi-LAT伽马射线观测，约束活动星系核冕区物理性质，并预测Seyfert星系群的弥散中微子通量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用TeV中微子信号探测活动星系核（如NGC 1068）最内层冕区物理性质，理解中微子产生机制与冕区湍流磁场、相干尺度等参数的关系。

Method: 通过联合拟合IceCube中微子和Fermi-LAT伽马射线观测数据，建立冕区湍流磁场模型，考虑伽马射线在光学厚环境中的吸收效应，约束NGC 1068和NGC 7469的冕区物理参数。

Result: NGC 1068倾向于紧凑、强磁化、湍流相干长度较大的冕区；NGC 7469指向较大尺寸、相干长度较小但磁化强度高、非热质子能量占比极小的冕区；提出了介于两者之间的第三种冕区模型以解释弥散中微子通量的谱转折。

Conclusion: 多信使观测为活动星系核冕区物理提供了定量约束，不同源表现出不同的冕区特性，模型预测已发布在GitHub仓库供进一步比较研究。

Abstract: The TeV neutrino signal observed by IceCube from the active galactic nucleus (AGN) NGC 1068 can probe its innermost coronal regions. If these neutrinos originate from hadrons accelerated within a magnetized turbulent corona, their intensity and spectrum depend on the turbulent magnetic field strength and turbulence coherence scale. The gamma rays accompanying neutrino production are absorbed in this optically thick environment, in a way that depends sensitively on the size of the corona. By a joint fit of the IceCube and Fermi-LAT observations, we translate the multimessenger signal from NGC 1068 and the tentative signal from NGC 7469 into quantitative constraints on coronal properties. NGC 1068, with a significant TeV neutrino excess, favors a compact, strongly magnetized corona with a large turbulence coherence length relative to the coronal size. NGC 7469, with two $\sim 100$ TeV neutrino events, points instead to a somewhat larger corona with much smaller coherence length and high magnetization, but a very small fraction of energy in non-thermal protons. We obtain the diffuse flux from a population of Seyfert galaxies identical to either NGC 1068 or NGC 7469. Finally, we consider a third scenario, motivated by the spectral break observed in the diffuse neutrino flux at tens of TeV, with coronal properties intermediate between the two point-source-inspired models. To enable detailed comparisons with the IceCube and electromagnetic observations, we release our model predictions in a GitHub repository.

</details>


### [138] [A systematic search for physical associations between fast radio bursts and astrophysical transients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18487)
*Hao-Hao Chen,Wen-Tao Xu,Xin-Yu Liang,Ming-Xuan Lu,Can-Min Deng*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究通过3D贝叶斯推断框架系统搜索快速射电暴（FRB）与高能天体瞬变（AT）的物理关联，使用3765个独特FRB样本，未发现新的统计显著关联，证实仅靠小角分离不足以确认关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速射电暴的物理起源仍是天体物理学未解之谜，磁星中心引擎模型是主流框架。系统搜索FRB与形成磁星的高能天体瞬变之间的物理关联，可为该模型提供关键检验，并为FRB前身星提供重要线索。

Method: 使用3765个独特FRB样本（结合第二版CHIME/FRB目录和124个具有红移测量的定位FRB），开发3D贝叶斯推断框架，联合考虑角分离、位置不确定性和红移约束来量化候选对的关联概率。通过空间交叉匹配识别14个FRB-光学瞬变和15个FRB-伽马射线暴候选对。

Result: 恢复了先前报道的FRB 20180916B与AT 2020hur的高显著性关联（关联概率0.9998）。对于候选对FRB 20190309A和短GRB 060502B，关联概率为0.83，不足以声称统计显著关联。所有其他候选对均未发现新的统计显著FRB-AT关联。

Conclusion: 仅靠小角分离不足以确认FRB-AT关联，高精度的FRB定位对于明确识别关联至关重要。研究强调了系统化方法在评估天体物理关联中的重要性。

Abstract: The physical origin of fast radio bursts (FRBs) remains an unsolved mystery in astrophysics, with the magnetar central engine model as the leading framework. Systematically searching for physical associations between FRBs and the energetic astrophysical transients (ATs) that form magnetars provides a critical test of this scenario, and key clues to FRB progenitors. We perform a systematic search for FRB-AT associations using a sample of 3765 unique FRBs, combining the second CHIME/FRB catalog with 124 additional localized FRBs with measured redshifts. We develop a 3D Bayesian inference framework that jointly incorporates angular separation, positional uncertainty, and redshift constraints to quantify the association probability of candidate pairs. Through spatial cross-matching, we identify 14 FRB-optical transient and 15 FRB-gamma-ray burst (GRB) candidate pairs. Our framework recovers the previously reported high-significance association between FRB 20180916B and AT 2020hur, with an association probability of 0.9998. For the proposed candidate FRB 20190309A and short GRB 060502B, our analysis yields an association probability of 0.83, which is insufficient to claim statistically significant association. No new statistically significant FRB-AT associations are found for all remaining candidates. Our work demonstrates that small angular separation alone is insufficient to confirm FRB-AT associations, and high-precision FRB localization is essential for definitive identification.

</details>


### [139] [GRB 241030A: a bright afterglow challenging forward shock emission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18956)
*J. -G. Ducoin,C. Pellouin,V. Aivazyan,D. Akl,F. Alvarez,C. Andrade,C. Angulo,S. Antier,J. -L. Atteia,S. Basa,R. L. Becerra,Z. Benkhaldoun,E. Bissaldi,A. Breeveld,E. de. Bruin,E. Burns,N. R. Butler,M. W. Coughlin,F. Daigne,T. Dietrich,D. Dornic,C. Douzet,T. du Laz,P. -A. Duverne,H. B. Eggenstein,E. Elhosseiny,A. Esamdin,P. A. Evans,J. F. Agüí Fernández,M. Ferro,F. Fortin,M. Freeberg,L. García-García,R. Gill,N. Globus,N. Guessoum,G. M. Hamed,P. Hello,A. Holzmann Airasca,D. F. Hu,T. Hussenot-Desenonges,R. Inasaridze,A. Iskandar,S. Q. Jiang,C. C. Jin,A. Kaeouach,S. Karpov,N. J. Klingler,A. Klotz,N. Kochiashvili,H. Koehn,R. Kneip,T. Kvernadze,A. Le Calloch,W. H. Lee,A. Lekic,Y. F. Liang,C. Limonta,J. Liu,K. Ocelotl. C. López,D. López-Cámara,R. H. Mabrouk,F. Magnani,J. Mao,M. Mašek,E. Moreno Méndez,B. M. Mihov,M. Molham,K. Noysena,M. Odeh,N. Omodei,H. Peng,M. Pereyra,M. Pillas,R. Pillera,T. Pradier,Y. Rajabov,N. A. Rakotondrainibe,B. Schneider,M. Serrau,L. Slavcheva-Mihova,O. Sokoliuk,H. Sun,A. Takey,M. Tanasan,K. S. Tinyanont,D. Turpin,A. de Ugarte Postigo,B. T. Wang,L. T. Wang,X. F. Wang,Z. M. Wang,A. M. Watson,H. Z. Wu,Q. Y. Wu,J. J. Xu,Y. S. Yan,H. N. Yang,W. Yuan,H. S. Zhao*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: GRB 241030A的余辉观测显示极端参数：极高动能、大喷流张角、低能电子和磁场能量分数，导致显著同步自康普顿辐射，与标准理论预期不符，表明余辉可能不完全由前向激波主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究GRB 241030A的明亮余辉，以探究伽马暴余辉物理，特别是通过多波段观测分析喷流结构和辐射机制。

Method: 收集从分钟到周尺度的多波段观测数据，通过统一测光流程处理，使用解析方法和贝叶斯推断，采用两个独立模型分析，假设余辉来自横向结构喷流的前向激波，可能包含同步自康普顿散射贡献。

Result: 模型成功拟合X射线到光学数据，支持喷流在恒定密度星际介质中传播，观测角位于喷流核心内。但参数值极端：极高动能（尽管瞬时辐射能量正常）、大喷流张角、低电子和磁场能量分数，导致显著的同步自康普顿辐射。

Conclusion: GRB 241030A属于具有极端动能、大喷流张角和极低瞬时辐射效率的伽马暴亚类，其参数难以与典型伽马暴预期协调，表明余辉可能不完全由前向激波辐射主导。

Abstract: Gamma-Ray Burst GRB 241030A (z = 1.411) exhibited a bright afterglow (similar to GRB 221009A), detected across gamma-ray, X-ray, UV, and optical bands, providing a probe of GRB afterglow physics. We compiled multi-wavelength observations spanning from a minute to a week after the prompt emission, processing the data through a unified photometry pipeline. We analysed the observations both analytically and using Bayesian inference with two independent models. Our models assume that the afterglow emission arises from the strong forward shock of a laterally structured jet, with possible contributions from synchrotron self-Compton (SSC) scatterings. Our models reproduce X-ray to optical data, favouring a jet propagating into a constant-density interstellar medium, with a viewing angle within the jet core. However, both analyses require parameter values that are extreme compared to expectations from standard theory. In particular, our results imply extremely energetic jets despite regular prompt energy, leading to a very inefficient prompt emission. Furthermore, the jets are inefficient at accelerating particles, with low electron and magnetic energy fractions, leading to significant SSC emission. Our analyses indicate that the jets have large opening angles and propagate in high-density media. If the afterglow is indeed powered by radiation emitted behind a strong forward shock, our results place GRB 241030A within a sub-class of GRBs characterised by extreme kinetic energies, large jet opening angles, and very low prompt emission efficiencies, with strong SSC radiation. These predictions are difficult to reconcile with typical expectations from other GRBs. We therefore suggest that the afterglow of GRB 241030A is not solely powered by forward shock emission.

</details>


### [140] [Non-Markovian Cosmic-Ray Pitch-Angle Transport from Mirror Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19037)
*Kai Yan,Huirong Yan,Parth Pavaskar,Chuanpeng Hou,Ruo-Yu Liu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究开发了包含磁镜漂移和回旋共振扩散的引导中心朗之万模型，用于描述宇宙射线在MHD湍流中的俯仰角输运，揭示了磁镜效应在大俯仰角处引发反常扩散，而回旋共振散射控制粒子从磁阱逃逸的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究宇宙射线在磁流体动力学湍流中的俯仰角输运机制，特别是磁镜效应和回旋共振散射之间的相互作用，以理解粒子在湍流磁场中的动力学行为。

Method: 开发了包含显式磁镜漂移和回旋共振扩散的引导中心朗之万模型，并通过三维MHD湍流中的测试粒子模拟进行验证，包括螺线管驱动和压缩驱动两种湍流模式。

Result: 磁镜效应可在包括90°的大俯仰角处驱动反常俯仰角扩散，具有非马尔可夫记忆效应；回旋共振散射控制粒子从磁阱的逃逸率；俯仰角输运主要由压缩扰动主导，阿尔芬模式影响较小；在考虑实际阻尼的压缩湍流中，传输时间阻尼处理能完全恢复磁镜相互作用。

Conclusion: 宇宙射线俯仰角输运是磁镜捕获和回旋共振逃逸共同调节的结果，大俯仰角粒子表现出从反常到正常扩散的转变，而小俯仰角粒子始终由回旋共振主导；压缩扰动在俯仰角输运中起主导作用。

Abstract: Cosmic-ray pitch-angle transport in magnetohydrodynamic (MHD) turbulence is governed by the interplay between magnetic mirroring and gyroresonant scattering. We develop a guiding-center (GC) Langevin model with explicit mirror drift and gyroresonant diffusion to describe the pitch angle evolution. This model accurately captures our test-particle simulation results in three-dimensional MHD turbulence, driven both solenoidally and compressively. We find that magnetic mirroring can drive anomalous pitch-angle diffusion at large pitch angles (including $90^\circ$) with non-Markovian memory effects, which arises from trapping of particles in magnetic wells. Gyroresonant scattering controls the escape rate from these wells. Across $M_{\rm A}$, large-pitch-angle particles are jointly regulated by mirror trapping and gyroresonant escape, exhibiting a transition from anomalous to normal diffusive pitch-angle transport as scattering strengthens, whereas small-pitch-angle particles remain gyroresonance-dominated and diffusive throughout. The pitch angle transport is found to be dominated by the compressible perturbations with marginal influence from Alfvén modes. In compressible turbulence with realistic damping accounted for, transit time damping (TTD) treatment fully recovers mirror interactions.

</details>


### [141] [GRB 240205B: A Reverse Shock Detected in Rapid Response Radio Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19047)
*S. I. Chastain,G. E. Anderson,A. J. van der Horst,L. Rhodes,C. Morley,A. Gulati,J. K. Leung,T. D. Russel,S. D. Ryder*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究利用ATCA和MeerKAT望远镜对GRB 240205B进行了宽带射电观测，获得了迄今为止最早的伽马射线暴射电余辉探测（约爆发后35分钟），揭示了早期反向激波成分，并给出了对最小体洛伦兹因子的最强模型独立约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在通过早期快速响应的射电观测，探测伽马射线暴余辉中的反向激波成分，并利用早期观测数据对暴发物理参数（特别是最小体洛伦兹因子）提供更强的约束。

Method: 使用澳大利亚望远镜致密阵列（ATCA）和南非MeerKAT射电望远镜进行宽带射电观测，包括自动触发的早期ATCA观测（爆发后约13分钟开始）和持续5个多月的后续观测。采用标准前向激波模型和结合反向激波的模型进行数据拟合。

Result: 1. 获得了迄今为止最早的GRB射电余辉探测（约爆发后35分钟）；2. 发现爆发后一天内的观测数据存在额外的同步辐射成分，可解释为反向激波；3. 结合反向和前向激波的恒星风介质模型能最好地描述射电余辉；4. 约束了同步辐射自吸收谱断点和最小电子能量；5. 利用光变曲线峰值约束了微观物理参数；6. 给出了对GRB最小体洛伦兹因子的最强模型独立约束（约100，在爆发后35分钟）。

Conclusion: 早期快速响应的射电观测对于探测GRB余辉中的反向激波成分至关重要，否则该成分可能被遗漏。研究展示了自动化快速触发观测的价值，为理解GRB物理机制提供了重要约束，并讨论了未来在厘米波段探测类似长暴及其改进观测策略的前景。

Abstract: Here we present broadband radio modeling of GRB 240205B, using observations with the Australia Telescope Compact Array (ATCA) and the South African MeerKAT radio telescope. Our observations include an automatically triggered early-time ATCA observation that began approximately 13 minutes after the gamma-ray signal and continued for 12 hours, resulting in the earliest detected GRB radio afterglow to date at about 35 minutes post-burst. Following this initial detection, we conducted an extensive radio follow-up campaign for more than 5 months. Although the observations beyond one day post-burst are well described by a standard forward shock model, the observation before one day post-bust reveals an additional synchrotron component, which can be explained as the reverse shock. This component would have been missed without the automated ATCA rapid-response trigger. We find that a combined reverse and forward shock model in a stellar wind medium best describes the radio afterglow. We constrain the spectral breaks due to synchrotron self-absorption and the minimum electron energy, and we use the light-curve peaks to constrain the microphysical parameters. We put GRB 240205B in the context of the growing sample of GRBs with radio detections in the first hours after the gamma-ray trigger. Using our rapid response observation, we estimate the highest model independent constraint on a GRB minimum bulk Lorentz factor of around 100 at about 35 minutes post burst. We also discuss future prospects of detecting similar long GRBs at centimeter wavelengths, as well as potential improvements to future strategies for targeting their radio afterglows.

</details>


### [142] [G objects as Primordial Black Hole-Neutron Star Remnants: Population Modeling and Multi-Wavelength Observables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19083)
*David Morales-Zapien,Stefano Profumo*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: G天体可能是中子星捕获原初黑洞后形成的低质量黑洞，而非恒星或气体云


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释银河系中心G天体的本质，这些天体具有紧凑的Brγ发射、极端红外颜色和通过中心超大质量黑洞时的动力学稳定性，挑战了传统恒星或非束缚气体云的解释

Method: 构建群体水平框架，将G天体的丰度和空间分布与中子星群体、内部暗物质密度分布、原初黑洞质量和丰度联系起来

Result: 观测到的G天体群体和银河系中心普通射电脉冲星的长期缺失是同一转换过程的互补结果；识别了红外、射电、X射线和微引力透镜等多波段观测特征

Conclusion: G天体可作为紧凑天体捕获物理和亚星系尺度暗物质的灵敏探针，该方案具有可观测验证性

Abstract: The nature of the so-called G objects orbiting the Galactic Center remains unresolved. These sources exhibit compact Br$γ$ emission, extreme infrared colors, and remarkable dynamical stability through close passages to the central supermassive black hole, challenging conventional interpretations as stars or unbound gas clouds. We investigate the hypothesis that G objects are the remnants of neutron stars that have been converted into low-mass black holes through the capture of primordial black holes, a viable dark-matter candidate. We construct a population-level framework linking the abundance and spatial distribution of these remnants to the neutron-star population, the inner dark-matter density profile, and the primordial black-hole mass and abundance. Within this framework, the observed G-object population and the long-standing deficit of ordinary radio pulsars in the Galactic Center emerge as complementary consequences of the same conversion process. We further identify a suite of observational signatures-across infrared, radio, X-ray, and microlensing channels-that render this scenario empirically testable and distinguishable from stellar-envelope models. Our results show that G objects can act as sensitive probes of compact-object capture physics and of dark matter on sub-galactic scales.

</details>

### [143] [Genetic determinism of circadian rhythm of feed intake and relation with feed efficiency evaluated in group-housed growing Large White pigs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18081)
*Lucile Riaboff,Ingrid David*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本研究通过时间-频率分析从电子饲喂站数据中提取了三个反映猪采食昼夜节律的新性状，发现这些性状具有中等遗传力，并与饲料效率存在有利的遗传相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管采食行为的遗传参数已被广泛研究，但群养自由采食生长猪在整个育肥阶段采食昼夜节律的遗传决定机制从未被研究过，尽管动物的昼夜节律与其代谢优化之间存在已知关系。

Method: 对2,297头大白猪的电子饲喂数据应用时间-频率分析方法，提出了三个新性状：反映整个育肥期采食昼夜节律一致性的DayCR，以及反映其建立早熟性的IndexCR和gCR。使用多性状模型估计这些性状的遗传力及其与剩余采食量的遗传相关。

Result: 三个昼夜节律性状显示中等遗传力（h²范围：0.24-0.35），剩余采食量具有高遗传力（0.41）。昼夜节律性状间存在高遗传相关（绝对值范围：0.87-0.98），表明在大多数育肥日（特别是后期）表现出24小时采食周期性的猪更早建立其昼夜节律。剩余采食量与昼夜节律性状之间存在低但有利的遗传相关（绝对值范围：0.18-0.27），表明具有一致且早期24小时采食周期性的动物也倾向于更高效利用饲料。

Conclusion: 本研究提出将时间-频率分析应用于纵向采食数据以检测生长猪育肥期间每小时采食模式的24小时周期性。结果表明，昼夜节律建立中观察到的部分变异受遗传驱动，进一步支持了对昼夜节律性状进行遗传选择的可行性。考虑到昼夜采食节律的生物学机制，选择能够表现出早期且一致的24小时采食周期性的动物可以促进代谢稳态，从而提高动物性能和抗逆性。

Abstract: Background Genetic parameters of feeding behaviours traits from electronic feeding stations in relation to feed efficiency have been widely explored. However, genetic determinism of the circadian rhythm of feed intake throughout the fattening phase in group-housed growing pigs fed ad libitum has never been investigated, despite the well-known relationships between animals' circadian rhythms and the optimization of their metabolism. The objective of this study was to (i) propose three new traits derived from time-frequency approach applied to electronic feeding data from 2,297 Large White pigs that reflect the consistency of circadian feed intake rhythm throughout fattening (so called DayCR) and the precocity of its establishment (so called IndexCR and gCR), and then to (ii) estimate the heritability of those traits and their genetic correlations with residual feed intake using a multiple trait model. Results Results highlighted moderate heritability estimates for the three circadian traits (range h2: [0.24; 0.35]) and high heritability for residual feed intake (0.41). High genetic correlations (range of absolute values: [0.87; 0.98]) among circadian traits suggested that pigs exhibiting a 24-hour periodicity in feed intake on most days of fattening, particularly during the final fattening period, establish their circadian rhythm earlier than the other pigs. The low (range of absolute values: [0.18; 0.27]) but favourable genetic correlations between residual feed intake and circadian traits revealed that animals with a consistent and early 24-hour periodicity of feed intake also tend to be more feed efficient. Conclusions This study proposed to apply time-frequency analysis on longitudinal feeding data to detect 24-hour periodicities in the hourly feed intake pattern across days throughout fattening in growing-pigs. Results suggested that part of the variability observed in the establishment of circadian rhythm is genetically driven, further supporting the feasibility of genetic selection on circadian traits. Considering the well-established biological mechanisms underlying circadian feeding rhythm, selecting animals for their ability to exhibit an early and consistent 24-hour periodicity of feed intake could promote metabolic homeostasis, thereby enhancing animal performance and resilience.

</details>


### [144] [Interplay between evolutionary and epidemic time scales challenges the outcome of control policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18801)
*Santiago Lamata-Otín,Alex Arenas,Jesús Gómez-Gardeñes,David Soriano-Paños*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 该论文提出了SIR模型的最小扩展，允许传染性演化，发现这种演化可导致疫情早期超指数增长、产生急剧的流行病转变，并削弱控制政策的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典SIR模型假设病原体特征固定，忽略了病毒演化。现实中病毒会进化，这会影响流行病动态和控制措施效果，需要建立包含演化过程的模型来理解这些影响。

Method: 提出了SIR模型的最小扩展，允许传染性演化。通过该模型分析演化对疫情动态的影响，推导了控制这种行为的临界突变率和干预时间的解析表达式。

Result: 演化可导致疫情早期超指数增长、产生急剧的流行病转变，并削弱控制政策效果（过早解除干预可能比不采取行动导致更严重的疫情）。发现了控制策略间的不对称性：缩短感染期仅阻碍传播而不抑制病毒演化，而降低传播率既能减少病例又能减缓病毒演化。

Conclusion: 病毒演化显著改变流行病动态，需要在流行病模型中考虑演化过程。控制策略的选择至关重要，降低传播率比缩短感染期更有效，因为它同时减少病例和减缓病毒演化。

Abstract: The SIR model is the cornerstone model for mathematical epidemiology, explaining key epidemic features such as the second-order transition between disease-free and epidemic states, the initial exponential growth of outbreaks or the short-term benefits of control measures. Nonetheless, the classical SIR model assumes that pathogen traits remain fixed, thus neglecting viral evolution. Here we propose a minimal extension of the SIR model, allowing infectiousness to evolve. We show that such evolution can cause superexponential early growth of outbreaks, create abrupt epidemic transitions, and undermine the effectiveness of control policies, as lifting interventions too early can lead to worse epidemic scenarios than no action. We derive analytical expressions for the critical mutation rate and intervention time governing this behavior, and identify a strong asymmetry between control strategies: while shortening the infectious period hinders transmission without suppressing viral evolution, lowering transmission both reduces cases and slows down viral evolution.

</details>

### [145] [ADAMOS: Axion Daily Modulation Searches for Dark Matter at 20 GHz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18006)
*Marios Maroudas,Toma-Stefan Cezar,Antonios Gardikiotis,Dieter Horns*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: ADAMOS项目提出在20GHz频率下使用薄壳设计腔体谐振器进行轴子暗物质搜索，利用14T超导磁体和校准RF链，同时探测三种轴子信号：传统冷暗物质轴子、相对论性轴子（日调制）和瞬态流暗物质增强信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索暗物质本质，特别是轴子暗物质。针对20GHz这一未充分探索的频率范围，基于先前实验经验改进设计，开发新型探测技术以开辟暗物质探测新通道。

Method: 采用固定频率（20GHz）腔体谐振器，创新性"薄壳"设计保持高频下的大探测体积。安装在现有14T超导磁体中，连接高灵敏度RF链并采用连续原位校准消除温度相关的增益漂移。

Result: 项目提出构建实验装置，将实现同时搜索三类轴子信号：1)传统冷暗物质轴子；2)轴子夸克块湮灭产生的相对论性轴子（呈现日调制）；3)流暗物质产生的瞬态增强信号。

Conclusion: ADAMOS将通过稳健、校准且多功能的实验装置，在先前未探索的暗物质参数空间区域开辟新的发现通道，推动轴子暗物质探测技术的发展。

Abstract: The ADAMOS (Axion Daily Modulation Searches) project aims to explore the nature of dark matter (DM) through a novel axion haloscope experiment. We propose to construct a fixed-frequency cavity resonator operating at 20 GHz at the University of Hamburg, using an innovative "thin-shell" design that preserves a large detection volume at high frequencies. The experiment will be installed in an existing 14 T superconducting magnet and connected to a highly sensitive RF chain with continuous in situ calibration to eliminate temperature-dependent gain drifts, constituting an essential improvement based on lessons learned from previous attempts. ADAMOS will conduct simultaneous searches for three classes of axion signals: (1) conventional cold DM axions, (2) relativistic axions from axion quark nugget annihilations exhibiting daily modulations, and (3) transient enhancements from streaming DM. By targeting this unexplored frequency regime with a robust, calibrated, and versatile setup, ADAMOS will open new discovery channels in a previously unexplored region of the dark sector.

</details>


### [146] [A search for lepton-flavour violating $τ\to 3μ$ decays with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18099)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: ATLAS实验在13 TeV质子-质子对撞中搜索τ→3μ轻子味破坏衰变，使用137 fb⁻¹数据，未发现信号，给出了分支比上限8.7×10⁻⁸（预期7.5×10⁻⁸）


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索带电轻子味破坏过程τ→3μ衰变，这是标准模型禁止的过程，其观测将明确指向新物理的存在，如超对称或轻子夸克等扩展模型

Method: 使用ATLAS探测器在13 TeV质子-质子对撞中收集的137 fb⁻¹数据，通过W→τν电弱产生通道，采用双μ子和三μ子触发，运用多变量分析分离信号与背景，最后对三μ子不变质量谱进行无分箱似然拟合

Result: 数据与仅背景假设兼容，未发现τ→3μ信号，观测到的分支比上限为8.7×10⁻⁸（90%置信水平），预期上限为7.5×10⁻⁸

Conclusion: 该研究为τ→3μ衰变设定了目前最严格的实验上限，为轻子味破坏过程提供了重要约束，有助于探索超出标准模型的新物理

Abstract: A search for charged lepton flavour violation in $τ\to 3μ$ decays is performed in $pp$ collisions at a centre-of-mass energy of 13 TeV using ATLAS data collected between 2016 and 2018, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 137 $\text{fb}^{-1}$. The search focuses on the electroweak $W \to τν$ production channel. Data are collected using two-muon and three-muon triggers and a multivariate analysis is used to separate the signal from the background. An unbinned likelihood fit is then performed to the resulting three-muon invariant mass spectrum and the data are found to be compatible with the background-only hypothesis. The observed (expected) limit on the branching ratio $B(τ\to3μ)$ is found to be $8.7\times 10^{-8}$ ($7.5\times 10^{-8}$) at $90\%$ CL.

</details>


### [147] [Reconstruction of overlapping electromagnetic showers in calorimeters using Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18172)
*Yuliia Maidannyk,Fabrice Couderc,Julie Malclès,Mehmet Özgür Sahin*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 深度学习聚类方法用于强子对撞机实验中电磁能量沉积的精确重建，包括两步法和单步图变换器ClusTEX，在重叠簇射重建、能量分辨率等方面优于标准算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代强子对撞机实验中，由于提升拓扑和堆积效应导致簇射重叠和能量分配模糊，需要精确的电磁能量沉积聚类来重建光子和电子。

Method: 提出了两种深度学习聚类方法：1）两步策略：先识别候选种子窗口，然后通过距离加权消息传递或注意力机制联合处理；2）单步图变换器ClusTEX：在单个推理阶段同时执行候选选择和重建，采用新颖的位置编码方案分离图内局部坐标和全局探测器坐标。

Result: 在玩具量能器中，基于注意力的交互改进了重叠簇射的重建，优于标准算法和距离驱动消息传递，同时保持孤立光子的性能并减少分裂。对于提升的π⁰→γγ，注意力模型保留了双光子质量重建能力。在ECAL启发拓扑中，ClusTEX提供最佳整体性能，能量分辨率改善，分裂减少，且在局部探测器故障下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 深度学习聚类方法特别是ClusTEX图变换器，在电磁量能器能量沉积重建中表现出优越性能，能够有效处理重叠簇射、改善能量分辨率、减少分裂，并在探测器故障情况下保持稳定性。

Abstract: Accurate clustering of electromagnetic energy deposits is essential for reconstructing photons and electrons in modern hadron collider experiments, where boosted topologies and pileup cause overlapping showers and ambiguous energy assignment. We present deep learning-based clustering approaches that reconstruct particle energy and position directly from calorimeter readout. The study includes a two-step strategy in which candidate seed windows are identified and then jointly processed via distance-weighted message passing or attention mechanism and a single-step graph transformer, ClusTEX, which performs candidate selection and reconstruction in one inference stage. ClusTEX uses a novel positional encoding scheme that separates local coordinates within the graph from global detector coordinates, enabling efficient, geometry-aware inference. Models are trained on GEANT4 simulations of a simplified (toy) and an ECAL-inspired topology with an explicit $η-φ$ dependence. Performance is evaluated using efficiency, energy and position resolutions and splitting rate - reconstruction of two objects for a single photon. In the toy calorimeter, attention-based interactions improve the reconstruction of overlapping showers relative to both the standard algorithm and distance-driven message passing, while maintaining performance on isolated photons and reducing splitting without multi-pass inference. For boosted $π^0\toγγ$, the attention-based model retains di-photon mass reconstruction capability, where the standard algorithm becomes inefficient. In the ECAL-inspired topology, ClusTEX provides the best overall performance, yielding improved energy resolution and reduced splitting compared to two-step approaches and the standard algorithm. It also remains robust under localized detector failures, showing improved stability and partial recovery of energy in non-responsive channels.

</details>

Zhou,A. N. Zhu,J. Zhu,K. Zhu,K. J. Zhu,K. S. Zhu,L. X. Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,J. H. Zou*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 首次观测到D_s^+ → π^+π^0π^0η衰变，测量了相关中间过程的支化比，为轻标量介子内部结构提供新约束


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究D_s^+介子衰变到多π和η的末态，探索轻标量介子（如a_0(980)和f_0(500)）的内部结构，验证同位旋对称性，并约束未探测的D_s^+ → ηX衰变分支比

Method: 使用BESIII探测器在质心能量4.128-4.226 GeV范围内收集的7.33 fb⁻¹ e⁺e⁻对撞数据，分析D_s^+ → π⁺π⁰π⁰η衰变过程，识别中间共振态并测量分支比

Result: 首次观测到D_s^+ → π⁺π⁰π⁰η衰变；测得D_s^+ → a_0(980)⁺ f_0(500)分支比为(0.98±0.16_stat±0.22_syst)%，显著性超过10σ；测得主导中间过程D_s^+ → a_1(1260)⁺η分支比为(1.77±0.21_stat±0.12_syst)%；验证了a_1(1260)⁺衰变到不同ρπ末态的同位旋对称性；测得非η'贡献的总分支比为(2.97±0.23_stat±0.14_syst)%，将未探测D_s^+ → ηX衰变分支比约束在(0.1±3.1)%

Conclusion: 该研究首次观测到D_s^+ → π⁺π⁰π⁰η衰变，为理解轻标量介子内部结构提供了重要实验约束，验证了同位旋对称性，并显著改善了未探测D_s^+ → ηX衰变分支比的限制

Abstract: We report the first observation of the decay $D_s^+ \to π^+π^0π^0η$ in a data set corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 7.33 fb$^{-1}$, collected in $e^+e^-$ collisions by the BESIII detector at center-of-mass energies between 4.128 and 4.226 GeV. An unexpectedly large branching fraction $\mathcal{B}( D_s^+ \to a_0(980)^+ f_0(500), a_0(980)^+ \to π^+η, f_0(500)\to π^0π^0) = (0.98 \pm 0.16_{\rm{stat.}} \pm 0.22_{\rm{syst.}})\%$ is measured with a significance exceeding $10σ$, offering new constraints on the internal structure of light scalar mesons. The dominant intermediate process is $D_s^+ \to a_1(1260)^+η, a_1(1260)^+\to ρ(770)^+π^0$ with a branching fraction of $(1.77 \pm 0.21_{\rm stat.} \pm 0.12_{\rm syst.})\%$. The isospin symmetry has been validated to the decays of $a_1(1260)^+\to ρ(770)^0π^+$ and $a_1(1260)^+\to ρ(770)^+π^0$. Moreover, the measured $\mathcal{B}(D_s^+\to π^+π^0π^0η|_{\rm{non}-η^\prime})=(2.97 \pm 0.23_{\rm stat.} \pm 0.14_{\rm sys.})$ reduces the undetected $D_s^+ \to ηX$ decay branching fractions to (0.1 $\pm$ 3.1)\%.

</details>


### [149] [Combined effective field theory interpretation of measurements sensitive to quartic gauge boson couplings in $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18630)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: ATLAS合作组利用13 TeV质子-质子对撞数据，通过矢量玻色子散射和三玻色子测量，对反常四重电弱规范玻色子耦合进行联合约束，使用有效场论框架下的Éboli模型维度-8算符参数化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模型中的电弱规范玻色子自相互作用由规范对称性决定，但超出标准模型的新物理可能引入反常耦合。通过测量矢量玻色子散射和三玻色子过程，可以探测这些反常四重耦合，从而寻找新物理迹象。

Method: 结合ATLAS探测器收集的√s=13 TeV质子-质子对撞数据，整合多个矢量玻色子散射终态分析以及三玻色子测量。使用有效场论框架，通过Éboli模型的维度-8算符参数化反常耦合，以Wilson系数表示。采用同时轮廓拟合方法，考虑单参数和双参数情况，并应用幺正性约束。

Result: 给出了68%和95%置信水平的区间限制，参数化为一个或两个Wilson系数，包含应用和不应用幺正性约束的情况。同时展示了幺正性和正定性理论界限，以及所有Wilson系数同时轮廓拟合得到的置信区间。

Conclusion: 该联合测量为反常电弱玻色子四重自耦合提供了严格的实验约束，这些结果对于检验标准模型电弱对称性破缺机制和寻找超出标准模型的新物理具有重要意义。

Abstract: A combination of measurements sensitive to anomalous quartic electroweak gauge boson couplings is presented using proton-proton collision data collected by the ATLAS detector at $\sqrt{s} = 13$ TeV at the LHC. Contributing analyses include measurements of vector-boson scattering in numerous final states as well as a tri-boson measurement. The combined measurement is used to constrain anomalous electroweak boson quartic self-couplings that result from dimension-8 operators in the Éboli model using an effective field theory. Results are presented as 68% and 95% confidence level intervals parameterised by one or two Wilson coefficients, both with and without unitarity constraints applied. Theoretical bounds from unitarity and positivity are overlaid where relevant. Confidence intervals obtained from simultaneous profiled fits to all Wilson coefficients are also presented.

</details>


### [150] [Measurement of the $\mathbf{B^0}$-meson production cross section in proton--proton collisions at $\mathbf{\sqrt{\textit{s}}=13.6}$ TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18904)
*ALICE Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: ALICE实验首次在LHC能量下测量了质子-质子对撞中B⁰介子的横向动量微分产生截面，测量范围延伸至p_T=1 GeV/c，结果与微扰QCD理论模型一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究重味强子（特别是B⁰介子）在质子-质子对撞中的产生机制，检验微扰量子色动力学（pQCD）理论模型在低横向动量区域的预测能力，为理解重夸克产生和碎裂过程提供实验数据。

Method: 使用ALICE探测器在√s=13.6 TeV的质子-质子对撞中，通过B⁰→D⁻π⁺衰变通道（随后D⁻→K⁺π⁻π⁻衰变）重建B⁰介子及其电荷共轭态，测量中快度区域（|y|<0.5）的横向动量微分产生截面，并与LHCb合作组在向前快度测量的B⁺介子数据进行比对。

Result: 首次在LHC能量下测量到p_T低至1 GeV/c的B⁰介子产生截面，测量结果与基于微扰QCD的先进理论模型在不确定度范围内一致。中快度单位快度产生截面为dσ(B⁰)/dy|_{|y|<0.5} = 24.2 ± 1.4(统计) ± 2.6(系统)_{-0.3}^{+0.2}(外推) μb。

Conclusion: 实验成功测量了B⁰介子在低横向动量区域的产生截面，验证了微扰QCD理论模型在描述重味强子产生方面的有效性，为理解重夸克产生机制提供了重要实验约束。

Abstract: This article reports the measurement of the transverse-momentum ($p_{\rm T}$) differential production cross section of B$^0$ mesons in proton-proton collisions at a centre-of-mass energy of $\sqrt{s}=13.6$ TeV with the ALICE detector at the CERN LHC. For the first time, the B$^0$ production cross section is measured at midrapidity ($|y|<0.5$) down to $p_{\rm T}=1~\mathrm{GeV}/c$ at LHC energies. The B$^0$ mesons and their charge conjugates were reconstructed via the B$^{0}\to$D$^{-}π^+$ decay channel, followed by the D$^-\to$K$^+π^-π^-$ decay. The measured $p_{\rm T}$-differential production cross section is described within uncertainties by state-of-the-art models based on perturbative quantum-chromodynamics calculations. Its rapidity dependence is also studied by computing the $p_{\rm T}$-differential ratios between the ALICE measurement and the one of B$^+$ mesons performed by the LHCb Collaboration at forward rapidity. The B$^0$ production cross section per unit of rapidity at midrapidity is ${\rm d}σ({\rm B^0})/{\rm d} y|_{|y|<0.5} = 24.2 \pm 1.4~(\text{stat.}) \pm 2.6~(\text{syst.})_{-0.3}^{+0.2}~(\text{extrap.})~μ{\rm b}$.

</details>
